Sources

(All sources cited are available in the Department of Legislative Services (DLS) Library and online as indicated.)

Legislative Drafting Resources

Bill Review Letters – An Analysis of Selected Bill Review Letters of the Attorney General of Maryland on Legislation Passed by the General Assembly.* DLS, Office of Policy Analysis. (This document was published annually from 1999 through 2020 and covers legislation passed during the immediately preceding legislative session. An electronic copy is available through the DLS Library’s online catalog.)

Compilation of the Changes in the Public Local Laws of Maryland.* DLS, Office of Policy Analysis. (This document was published annually through 2019 and describes the current status of public local laws. Electronic copies dating back to 1998 are available through the DLS Library’s online catalog.)

Issue Papers.* DLS, Office of Policy Analysis. (Issue briefs on matters likely to be considered during upcoming legislative session. They are published annually in the fall prior to session. Electronic copies dating back to 1998 are available through the DLS Library’s online catalog.)

Legislative Desk Reference Manual. DLS, Office of Policy Analysis, 2023. (Black letter statements of law relevant to legislative drafting, e.g., local government powers; “one subject” rule; federal preemption; rules of legislative procedure. An electronic copy is available through the DLS Library’s online catalog.)

Legislative Handbook Series.* DLS, Office of Policy Analysis, 2026. (9 volumes: Vol. I, Maryland Legislator’s Handbook; Vol. II, Government Services in Maryland; Vol. III, Maryland’s Revenue Structure; Vol. IV, Maryland’s Budget Process; Vol. V, Maryland State Personnel, Pensions, and Procurement; Vol. VI, Maryland Local Government; Vol. VII, Business Regulation in Maryland; Vol. VIII, Maryland’s Criminal and Juvenile Justice Process; Vol. IX, Education in Maryland. This series is published once every four years. An electronic copy of each volume also is available through the DLS Library’s online catalog.)

Major Issues Review.* DLS, Office of Policy Analysis, 2026. (Published every four years, summarizing legislative activity over the four-year term. Includes 324 Legislative Drafting Manual – 2026 a discussion of all major enacted legislation, significant bills that did not pass, and gubernatorial vetoes of major legislation. An electronic copy is also available through the DLS Library’s online catalog.)

Maryland Clipper. DLS, Library and Information Services. (Compilation of news clippings on issues of interest to the General Assembly, arranged by subject. Published electronically daily during session and weekly during the interim. Available in Microsoft Teams: DLS/Maryland Clipper.)

Maryland Documents Catalog. DLS, Library and Information Services. (Online catalog compilation of all State and local publications received and processed by the DLS Library. The State and local publications also are available in the DLS Library. The library previously compiled the documents into a monthly publication. Electronic copies of the monthly publications from 2007 through the March 2026 are available through the DLS Library’s online catalog.)

Maryland Manual. Maryland State Archives (Biographies of State officials;

organization of Maryland government; addresses, telephone numbers, maps, statistics, etc. Updated daily.) Maryland Ongoing and Annual Reporting Requirements. DLS, Library and Information Services, 2026. (Compilation of all annual and ongoing mandated reports published every four years starting in 2018. The document may be accessed online through the DLS Library’s online catalog.)

Maryland Style Manual for Statutory Law. DLS, Office of Policy Analysis, 2018. (See discussion infra, p. 37. An electronic copy is available through the DLS Library’s online catalog.)

Model Guide for Drafting Governmental Units and Licensing Provisions. DLS, 2008.

(An electronic copy is available through the DLS Library’s online catalog.)

The 90 Day Report.* DLS, Office of Policy Analysis. (Published at the conclusion of each annual legislative session. Discusses legislation passed by the General Assembly with an overview of legislative activity arranged by subject area. Successor to Session Review and Sine Die Report, which were published annually by the former Dept. of Legislative Reference and the former Dept. of Fiscal Services from 1985 through 1997. Electronic copies of The 90 Day Report from 2001 through 2026 are available through the DLS Library’s online catalog. Note that, due to the shortened session in 2020, the 2020 issue is titled The 71 Day Report.) Appendix 325 Researching the U.S. and Maryland Constitutions: Some Basic Sources. Compiled by Michael S. Miller. Maryland State Law Library, 1987.

Rules of the Maryland House of Delegates. Chief Clerk of the House of Delegates, 2026.

Rules of the Maryland Senate. Secretary of the Senate, 2026.

Summary Reports of Committees to the Legislative Policy Committee. DLS, Office of Policy Analysis. (An annual compilation of the summary reports of the standing committees, special joint subcommittees, joint statutory committees, and special joint legislative committees that function during the interim. Electronic copies starting in 2017 are available through the DLS library’s online catalog. Print copies starting in 1998 are available from the DLS library.)

Division of State Documents. (Provides online version of the Code of Maryland Regulations (COMAR) and the Maryland Register.)

United States Code (USC) Annotated (USCA). (A consolidation and codification by subject matter of the general and permanent laws of the United States.)

Government Accountability Office. (Examines the use of public funds, evaluates federal programs and activities, and provides analyses, options, recommendations, and other assistance to help Congress make effective oversight, policy, and funding decisions.)

Ghost Hunting: Searching for Maryland Legislative History. Michael S. Miller, 1998.
